通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果
2026 年 10 月 15 日
一、 市場動態
1. 科技股領跌，費半連 6 日收黑：美股主要指數 10 月 14 日全數下跌，道瓊工業指數下
跌 0.09%，收 51,621.48 點；S&P 500 指數下跌 1.11%，收 7,718.94 點，為 7 月 29
日以來最大單日跌幅；Nasdaq 綜合指數下跌 1.88%，收 26,692.87 點，為 7 月 23
日以來最大單日跌幅。費城半導體指數下跌 4.30%，收 11,684.38 點，連續第６個交
易日走低，為 2025 年 5 月以來最長連跌紀錄。

2. 類股非全面下挫，資金仍呈輪動現象：半導體及 AI 相關股票在 10/14 跌幅尤其明顯
，AMD 下跌 5.73%、Nvidia 下跌 3.16%、Broadcom 下跌 3.10%，AI 基礎建設相關
的 CoreWeave、Nebius、Oracle 及 Dell 跌幅更深，顯示市場對資本支出規模較大、
融資成本敏感度較高的 AI 相關企業風險偏好明顯下降。不過，S&P 500 的 11 大類
股中有 8 類下跌，資訊科技及通訊服務為主要拖累來源，但醫療保健、必需消費與能
源類股仍逆勢上漲。
3. 美國 10 年期公債殖利率盤中急升後回落：美國 10 年期公債殖利率 10/14 由 5.2%左
右快速升至約 5.33%，逼近近期高點；然而，隨後市場消化 Fed 官員較審慎的政策訊
號，殖利率自高檔回落，終場約收於 5.19%，較前一交易日 5.21%小幅下降約 2 個基
點。
二、 下跌原因分析
1. 美 9 月通膨高於預期，Fed 再升息風險重新升高：美國 10/14 公布 9 月 CPI 月增 0.6%

、年增率也由 8 月的 3.4%上揚至 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%、年增率由 8 月的 2.4%
升至 2.6%。其中汽油價格單月大漲 9.1%，貢獻整體 CPI 月增幅超過一半；住宅成本
亦月增 0.4%，顯示物價壓力仍具黏性。此數據公布後，市場重新提高 Fed 進一步升
息的預期，CME FedWatch 所反映的 10 月升息 1 碼機率一度由前一日約 22%提高至
37%。雖然截至 10/15 台北時間早上 7 點左右該機率回降至 29.3%，這也提醒投資人
Fed 的升息路徑仍存在不確定性。
2. 高利率同時壓縮科技股估值與提高 AI 融資成本：科技及半導體公司未來獲利成長占評
價的比重較高，因此對折現率變化相對敏感。當長天期美債殖利率維持 5%以上，市
場願意支付的本益比自然受到限制，即使企業獲利仍維持成長，股價也可能因評價倍
數下降而出現修正。另一方面，本輪 AI 投資需要大量資料中心、伺服器、電力與網路
基礎設施，高利率亦直接增加企業融資成本，反映信用市場對高度依賴外部資金的 AI

基礎建設公司要求更高風險溢酬。

確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決
策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. AI 投資焦點由「支出規模」轉向「投資報酬率」:近期市場對 AI 的疑慮逐漸由「需求
是否成長」，轉向「龐大的資本支出能否轉化為足夠營收、獲利與自由現金流」。
Broadcom 執行長陳福陽近期亦指出，AI 未來發展的重要限制可能逐漸由晶片或記憶
體供應轉向資本本身。我們認為，這並不代表 AI 資本支出週期即將結束，而是市場已
由第一階段的「資本支出愈大、成長想像愈高」，逐步進入更重視資本效率與投資報酬
率的第二階段。未來財務結構、現金流與 AI 商業化能力，將成為不同企業評價分化的
重要因素。
4. 中國記憶體競爭疑慮加重半導體短線壓力：中國長鑫存儲（CXMT）10/10 傳出記憶

體技術取得進展，引發市場對未來 DRAM 供給增加及價格競爭的疑慮，記憶體相關類
股率先承壓，並進一步影響整體半導體市場情緒。惟 DRAM 與 GPU、客製化 ASIC、
先進晶圓製程及先進封裝的產業競爭與需求結構並不相同。我們認為，儘管記憶體競
爭升溫值得關注，但目前尚不足以延伸為整體 AI 半導體需求同步轉弱。
5. 高檔籌碼加速獲利了結：AI 與半導體仍是 2026 年美股表現最強勢的投資主題之一，
部分科技股今年累計漲幅已相當可觀。當通膨、殖利率及 AI 投資報酬疑慮同時升高，
先前漲幅較大、投資人部位較集中的族群自然成為優先調節對象。因此，本波費半快
速修正除了總經與基本面疑慮外，亦包含籌碼降溫及風險部位收縮因素，使短期價格
波動幅度可能高於企業基本面實際變化。
三、市場看法
1. 類股輪動明顯，尚非全面看空美股：科技與 AI 相關股票 10 月 14 日明顯承壓，但醫
療保健、必需消費與能源類股仍逆勢上漲，道瓊工業指數僅下跌 0.09%。此外，S&P
500 成分股下跌家數與上漲家數之比約為 1.1：1；相較於指數下跌 1.11%，市場廣度

並未同步大幅惡化。這反映資金主要由高評價且前期漲幅較大的科技與 AI 族群，轉向
現金流較穩定的防禦型產業。現階段市場特徵較接近類股輪動與風格切換，而非無差
別的全面風險撤離，亦尚未看到美股企業獲利或經濟基本面全面反轉的明確訊號。
2. 美股科技股財報前風險降載，Q3 仍存在重新定價空間：近期科技股修正，費城半導
體指數連續６個交易日下跌，也可能部分反映第三季財報季正式展開前，投資人在股
價先前漲幅較大、評價偏高及利率環境不確定下，提前降低風險曝險。真正決定後續
市場方向的關鍵，仍在於實際企業獲利能否支持目前的高成長預期。根據 Bloomberg
季度獲利成長追蹤資料，今年 Q1 與 Q2 財報季期間的 EPS 成長率經驗顯示，隨實際
財報陸續公布，企業獲利成長曾明顯優於財報季開始前的市場預期。儘管這並不保證
Q3 將複製前兩季的上修幅度，但目前市場因財報不確定性所反映的風險折價仍存在
被實際財報重新驗證的可能。若大型科技及半導體公司後續營收、獲利與 AI 需求指引
實際公布結果優於預期，近期風險溢價可望部分收斂；反之，若獲利成長無法跟上目

前評價，市場整理時間則可能進一步延長。

不另行通知。
3. 費半修正幅度大，但 AI 需求尚未出現全面反轉：ASML 在 10/14 所公布的最新第三
季財報反而顯示 AI 相關設備需求仍相當強勁，該公司表示 2027 與 2028 年規劃產能
可能仍無法完全滿足客戶需求，部分客戶亦已表達對 2029 及 2030 年設備需求的興
趣。儘管費城半導體指數近期市場情緒轉弱，但由 ASML 的財報表現來看，目前仍未
看到大型 AI 客戶全面取消晶片訂單、主要雲端服務商同步削減 AI 資本支出，或先進
製程需求快速降溫等結構性訊號。
4. 台積電營收顯示 AI 需求暢旺，留意 10/15 法說會內容：台積電 10/8 公布的第三季營

收達新台幣 1.49 兆元、年增約 50%，創歷史新高，並高於市場原先預估的 1.46 兆元
；9 月單月營收年增 54.6%。強勁的營收表現仍反映 AI 與先進製程需求具有支撐。目
前市場因此呈現「企業營收仍快速成長，但股票評價下降」的狀況。高殖利率環境下
，EPS 上升與本益比下降可以同時發生，後續股價方向將取決於企業獲利上修幅度是
否足以抵銷評價收縮。該公司將於 10/15 下午舉行第三季法人說明會。市場重點將集
中於第四季展望、AI 與 HPC 需求、先進製程、先進封裝及未來資本支出規劃。公司
對 AI 需求與產能投資的最新判斷，將成為判斷近期費半修正主要屬於評價調整，或產
業基本面是否開始出現變化的重要依據。
5. AI 與半導體進入基本面分化階段：我們認為，在高利率環境下，高度仰賴外部融資、
自由現金流尚未成熟的 AI 基礎建設業者，受到的資金成本壓力明顯高於具穩定現金流
的大型科技與雲端服務商。同樣地，記憶體、GPU、ASIC、先進製程及先進封裝各有
不同供需結構。我們預期下一階段 AI 行情將更加重視訂單能見度、產品競爭力、毛利

率、自由現金流及 AI 營收實際兌現，產業內部強弱分化程度可能進一步提高。
6. Fed 路徑仍存不確定性，惟企業獲利可望提供股市支撐：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使市場重
新評估 Fed 後續升息時點與幅度，美國利率政策仍存在不確定性，長天期美債殖利率
也可能維持高檔。不過，現階段企業獲利成長與 AI 相關需求仍具韌性，若第三季財報

延續前兩季優於預期的趨勢，企業 EPS 若能上修有望部分抵銷高利率對本益比的壓縮
效果。換言之，後續市場表現未必只由 Fed 政策主導，企業實際獲利能否持續兌現，
將是支撐科技與半導體股中期走勢的重要力量。
四、投資建議
1. 短線聚焦美股財報與 Fed 決策，科技股中期趨勢料不致有變：近期市場同時面臨財報
前的觀望態勢、美債殖利率偏高及 AI 資本支出疑慮等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能
受到壓抑，後續宜關注 10 月美股財報對企業獲利與資本支出展望，以及 FOMC 會議
所釋出的政策訊號。中期而言，目前 AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡

象，科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，但在利率維持高檔及市場評價偏高的環境下，後
續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出的實際變化。
2. 中天期債券風險報酬仍較均衡：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使短端殖利率持續受到 Fed 政策路
徑影響，而 20～30 年超長端仍面臨財政赤字、國債供給與期限溢價偏高等結構性壓
力。相較之下，目前殖利率已位於歷史相對高檔，若後續核心通膨或能源價格改善，
中段殖利率具有回落條件，同時其存續期間風險又低於超長端，因此現階段以中天期
美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國聯準會如市場預期，維持政策利率不變。決策官員仍對通膨保持擔憂，也承
認美國經濟正以「強勁速度」成長。聯準會主席 Kevin Warsh 在會後談話中，並
未明確排除進一步緊縮政策的可能，激勵市場對 12 月升息的預期升溫。數據方
面，美國第三季 GDP 年化成長率達 3.4%，高於前值的 2.2%，亦優於市場預期
的 3.2%，印證美國經濟動能強勁。此外，9 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 符合預期，但凸
顯通膨壓力持續偏高。在「通膨高企」與「經濟強韌」雙重因素驅動下，市場預
期聯準會將於 12 月升息，為美元指數提供有力支撐。惟近期受 AI 產業股票波
動加劇影響，部分資金轉入美債避險，導致美元指數在高檔面臨調節性賣壓。
⚫ 儘管受能源價格走揚影響，9 月歐元區通膨率升至 3.8%，但在薪資壓力維持溫
和，且歐洲央行尚未觀察到第二輪通膨效應全面擴散的情況下，歐洲央行上週決
議維持政策利率不變。通膨回升令市場預期歐洲央行將在下次會議升息，為歐元

提供部分支撐，但考量歐元區經濟成長表現仍落後美國，在美元基本面強勁的抑
制下，歐元反彈力道相對有限。目前歐元最大的潛在風險在於部分國家的債券市
場壓力尚未消散，市場對歐洲財政風險仍保持高度警戒。
⚫ 日本央行維持政策利率於 1.25%不變，並且首次明確表示已達成其 2%的通膨目
標；過去日本央行雖認為通膨改善，但始終強調尚未穩定達標，如今首次正式承
認達成 2%目標，代表未來升息門檻進一步下降。此外，日本央行將經濟成長預
測維持在 0.7%至 0.8%，並小幅上修 CPI 預測，令市場預期日央 12 月有望再次
升息，日本貨幣政策正常化將有助日圓結束疲軟走勢。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.390，低點在 32.676，以 32.478 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 3 分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，市場等待聯準會利率決議，交投偏謹慎觀望，而外
資在前周大幅匯出之後，轉為雙向進出，台幣止跌回穩，最高來到 32.390 的周內高
點；然而周間，美國科技股財報表現失色，台股賣壓沉重，外資賣超台股並再度轉
為大幅匯出，台幣走貶，最低來到 32.676，不過匯價逢高吸引出口商賣壓出籠，加
上央行提供流動性，台幣貶幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.478。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣偏弱震盪。就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持利率不
變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，且聯準會表示通膨仍處於高檔，顯示聯準會對通
膨仍保持警戒，市場預期美國利率將維持高檔，台美利差仍維持在相對高位，不利
台幣表現；另一方面，美國科技股財報表現不如預期，引發市場對 AI 投資回報的疑
慮，對台股也帶來壓力。就台灣市場來看，外資持續調節台股並大幅匯出，10 月累
計賣超將近兆元，資金匯出壓力大，不過一方面央行持續進場調節，加上匯價逢高
預計吸引出口商進場拋匯，料將限制台幣貶值速度。綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢
偏弱。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣持續走升。周初市場等待美國聯準會利率決議，交投偏向觀望，境
外人民幣主要於 6.6600 上方震盪；周間聯準會如預期維持利率不變，美元指數自 102
上方回落，加上中國出口結匯需求持續提供支撐，境外人民幣突破 6.6600 整數關口；
週五人行中間價續創近三年九個月新高，最高來到 6.7138，顯示中國人行允許人民
幣溫和升值的走勢，境外人民幣一度升破 6.6500 整數關口，最高來到 6.6487，終場
收在 6.6528，週線升值 162 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6558，週線升值 146 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣偏升。就中國國內市場來看，10 月官方製造業 PMI 降至 49.3，
重新落入景氣收縮區間，反映中國內需及製造業動能仍有待改善，不過 9 月出口年
增 36.3%，出口商結匯需求持續提供人民幣支撐，加上人行中間價續創近三年九個
月新高，預計將持續帶動人民幣走升；就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持
利率不變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，顯示聯準會對通膨仍保持警戒，市場預期
美國利率將維持高檔，美中利差維持在相對高位，將限制人民幣升值的速度與幅度；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線向下發散，匯價維持在五日均
線之下交投，均線呈現空頭排列，顯示空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣
偏升。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6200-6.6700

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 上漲。週初，美伊衝突仍陷僵局支撐美元避險買盤，USD/JPY 最
高來到 158.34；週間，聯準會公布利率維持利率不變，USD/JPY 持續在 158 附近盤
整；臨近週末，日本央行公布利率維持不變並釋放偏鷹訊號，USD/JPY 最低來到
156.94，終場收在 157.53，週線上漲 72 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於上周五宣布利
率維持在 1.25%不變，並表示隨著基礎通膨率大致達到 2%，央行必須持續控制通
膨穩定於此目標，且再次強調貨幣政策已進入更為緊縮階段，未來每隔數月即可能
升息。此外，日本央行在季度報告中，亦將截至 2028 年 3 月財年的核心消費者通
膨預估自三個月前預測的 2.4%上調至 2.6%。日本央行偏鷹立場令市場預期日本最
快將於 12 月再次升息，預計將有利日圓表現。二、聯準會宣布維持利率於 3.75%-
4%區間不變，惟因通膨仍處高位，聯準會重申將致力實現 2%的長期通膨區間， 
FedWatch 顯示 12 月升息機率約七成，美元持穩，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 下行空
間。三、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 各天期均線趨平，近期匯價大致
在 156 至 158 間震盪，匯價暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現
區間震盪的可能性較高。

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美伊衝突持續以及法國政經情勢動盪等情況下，
歐元走跌，最低來到 1.1111；週間，聯準會和歐洲央行相繼公布利率決議均維持不
變，歐洲央行發表言論偏鷹派，歐元止跌回升至 1.1214；臨近週末，歐元區公布經
濟數據好壞參半，歐元自高點回落，終場收在 1.1158，週線下跌 41 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行如市場預期宣布
存款利率維持在 2.5%不變，並警告能源成本持續上漲將對通膨造成上行風險，暗示
可能在 12 月再次升息，升息預期升溫預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、歐元區第三季 GDP
年增 1.3%，高於市場預期的 1.1%，顯示在能源價格持續上漲的不利因素下，經濟仍
展現韌性，預計有利歐元表現。三、聯準會宣布維持利率在 3.75%-4%不變，並重申
致力控制通膨的立場。此外，美國公布第三季 GDP 成長 3.4%，優於市場預期的 3.2%，
9 月核心 PCE 年增率自前月的 3%上升至 3.1%，在美國經濟強勁及通膨仍高的情況
下，市場預期聯準會將於 12 月升息一碼，美元持穩，預計將限縮歐元走升空間。四、
技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 各天期均線向下發散，不過近期匯價在多次向
下測試 1.1120 附近均未有效跌破，顯示下方仍有買盤支撐。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD
本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

1 
2606 裕民 ( U-MING )

報告日期：2026/11/17 研 究 員：林奕頎(Yichi Lin) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
報告重點：預估 4Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 2.34 元，YoY + 51% 
旺季，10 月營收 YoY+57%，11 月及 12 月 YoY 仍強。 
1.預估 4Q / 2026 單季稅後 EPS + 2.34 元，創最近 67 季新高 
裕民公告 2026 年 10 月 單月營收 YoY + 51%，創歷史新高。 
宏遠基於保守原則，估裕民 11 月營收 18.50 億元； 12 月營
收 19.05 億元； 估 4Q 單季稅後 EPS + 2.34 元，YoY + 51%。 
若裕民 11 月 及 12 月營收 高於預期，則須上修 4Q 獲利。 
2.BDI 處於獲利水準，裕民 2027 年增加 6 艘新船，將受惠 
2026 年 前三季 BDI 平均 2,586；裕民前三季稅後 EPS 5.80
元。 2026 年 11 月 13 日 BDI 2,980，仍處於明顯獲利水準。 
裕民 2027 年新增 6艘船：輕便型 3艘 + 海岬船 2艘 + 1 艘 
高階海事工程船（用於風力發電工程）； 裕民總船數從 2026
年 72 艘，增加至 2027 年底 78 艘。 
3.海運機構估計 2026 年供給過剩，實際上，BDI 大漲 64% 
海運機構預期 2026 年散裝航運供給過剩，但 2026 年（ 截 至 11
月 13 日）BDI 運價指數平均 2,615，遠高於 2025 年同期平均
值 1,597，高出 64%； 很明顯，海運機構的預測值，忽略某些
關鍵性因素，導致預測結果出現偏差。 
目前海運研究機構 BIMCO，預估 2027 年散裝航運供給成長率
（ + 3.5% ~ + 4.5% ），將超過需求成長率（ + 0.5% ~ + 
1.5% ），延續 2026 年供給過剩的看法。 
宏遠基於保守原則，暫時採用 BIMCO 看法： 
(1)假設 2027 年 BDI 運價下跌，裕民 2027 年毛利率 + 28.8%，
將明顯低於 2026 年毛利率 + 35.3%，下滑 6.5 個百分點。 
(2)裕民依照造船計畫，2027 年新增 6 艘新船。 
宏遠估計裕民 2027 年稅後 EPS + 6.51 元，將低於 2026 年預
估值 + 8.14 元； 若 2027 年毛利率下滑幅度，低於 6.5 個百
分點，則必須上調裕民 2027 年獲利預估值。 
4.買進：26 年 EPS 創 18 年新高； 高殖利率行情，將激勵股價 
裕民 2026 年前三季稅後 EPS + 5.80 元，10 月單月營收創歷史
新高； 宏遠預估 裕民 2026 年稅後 EPS + 8.14 元，將創最近
18 年新高；同時，估計 2027 年 3 月公告每股現金股利，將介
於 4.7 ~ 6.0 元，將有高殖利率題材 ； 建議買進。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況 
一.公司簡介 
截至 2026 年 9 月（法說資料），裕民實際經營船隻為 72 艘： 
海岬型船  25 艘：自有 22 艘 + 3 艘合資。 
巴拿馬型船 24 艘：自有 14 艘 + 7 艘代管 + 3 艘合資。 
輕便型     8艘（自有）。 
水泥船     4艘（自有）。 
油輪       4艘：透過 環能海運（持股 40%）。 
CTV 離岸風電人員運輸船 6 艘（自有 ； 裕民風能航運）。 
天然氣船   1 艘（持有 50%；與日本 K LINE 合資）。

在建船隻 14 艘，交船時程： 
2026 年：輕便型 1 艘（預期 11 月交付）。 
2027 年：輕便型 3 艘+ 海岬船 2 艘+ 高階海事工程船 1 艘。 
2028 年：輕便型 2 艘+ 海岬船 2 艘+ 高階海事工程船 1 艘。 
2030 年：海岬船 2 艘。

二.產業趨勢概況 
1.海運機構預期 2026 年供給過剩； 實際上，BDI 走勢強勁 
雖然，先前，多家海運專業研調機構，都預測 2026 年全球
散裝航運，供給成長率超過需求成長率，將出現供給過剩的
狀況。

實際上，2026 年截至 11 月 13 日，散裝 BDI 運價走勢都優於
2025 年同期表現（參閱圖 1） 。

受惠於 BDI 的強勁走勢，裕民： 
1Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 1.17 元，優於 1Q / 2025 同期 + 0.27 元。 
2Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 2.34 元，優於 2Q / 2025 同期 + 0.77 元。 
3Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 2.29 元，優於 3Q / 2025 同期 + 1.72 元。 
裕民公告 2026 年 10 月 單月營收 創歷史新高。

從 BDI 的表現來看： 
1Q / 2026（過年淡季）BDI 平均 1,955。 
2Q / 2026（美伊戰爭爆發，燃煤替代天然氣）BDI 平均 2,751。 
3Q / 2026  BDI 平均 3,041。 
2026 年 11 月 13 日 單日 BDI  2,980。 
2025 年 4Q 旺季 BDI 高點 2,845。

4Q 是散裝航運的季節性旺季，遇上， 荷姆斯海峽封閉 推升 
煤炭的海運需求  及   巴拿馬運河缺水 降低 海運船舶供
給（繞道等同減少供給）； 預期 4Q BDI 的 YoY 表現，將會
持續強勁。

BDI 的 YoY 漲勢強勁，宏遠預期裕民 4Q / 2026 單季獲利 YoY + 
51% ； 未來，4Q / 2026 財報發布，將激勵股價表現。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估 
1.估 2026 年稅後 EPS + 8.14 元，YoY + 89%

先前，多家國際海運研調機構，預期 2026 年散裝航運供給過
剩，但 2026 年（截至 11 月 13 日）BDI 運價指數平均 2,615，
遠高於 2025 年同期平均值 1,597，高 出 64%（參閱圖 1）； 很
明顯，海運機構的預測值，忽略某些關鍵性因素，導致預測結
果出現偏差。

裕民公告 1Q / 2026 財報營收 38.92 億元，YoY + 13%。 
稅後 EPS + 1.17 元，YoY + 335%。 
公告 2Q / 2026 財報營收 50.14 億元，YoY + 39%。 
稅後 EPS + 2.34 元，YoY + 204%。 
公告 3Q / 2026 財報營收 52.78 億元，YoY + 23%。 
稅後 EPS + 2.29 元，YoY + 33%。 
公告 2026 年 10 月單月營收 21.77 億元，YoY + 57%，新高。

裕民 2026 年 11 月 將下水 1艘 輕便型船。

宏遠估計，裕民 2026 年： 
營收 201.16 億元，YoY + 23%；

營業利益 61.96 億元，YoY + 67% ；

業外淨收支 + 8.43 億元，YoY + 503%； 
權益法轉投資收益改善 及 金融資產評價利益增加。

稅後純益 68.75 億元，YoY + 89% ；

稅後 EPS + 8.14 元，優於 2025 年財報稅後 EPS + 4.31 元。

預估 裕民  
2026 年稅後 EPS 預估值 
8.14 元 
創最近 18 年新高 
（2009 年 ~ 2026 年）

2.估 2027 年稅後 EPS + 6.51 元，YoY - 20% 
假設：(1) 海運研調機構預測， 2027 年全球散裝航運過剩。 
2027 年 BDI 下跌。 
截至目前（參閱圖 1），BDI 的 YoY 漲勢仍是強勁的。 
(2) 假設 BDI 下跌，裕民 2027 年毛利率下滑 6.5 個百分點。 
(3) 裕民依照訂船規劃，2027 年新增 6艘船舶。

宏遠估計，裕民 2027 年： 
營收 220.56 億元，YoY + 10%； 
2026 年 11 月 下水 1艘新船； 
2027 年 下水 6艘新船； 
成為 2027 年營收上升的主因。

營業利益 54.17 億元，YoY - 13%； 
BDI 下跌，造成 毛利率 及 營利率 下滑。

業外淨收支 + 3.59 億元，YoY - 57%； 
2026 年認列金融資產評價利益。 
2027 年配合新船下水，利息費用上升。

稅後純益 55.04 億元，YoY - 20%；

稅後 EPS + 6.51 元，低於 2026 年稅後 EPS 預估值 + 8.14 元。

多家海運研調機構 
延續 2026 年 供給過剩看法 
預估 2027 年 持續過剩

基於保守原則 
宏遠假設 
2027 年 BDI 下跌 
裕民 2027 年毛利率 
下滑 6.5 個百分點 
成為預估獲利衰退的主因

若後續 
BDI 沒有重挫 
裕民 2027 年毛利率沒有大減 
則必須明顯上修獲利預估

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
In this report, we focus on preliminary JMTBA orders, which we
believe could be a catalyst for AirTAC's and Hiwin's share prices.
According to the Japan Machine Tool Builders' Association (JMTBA), headline

orders dropped 8% MoM but grew 43% YoY in October  2026 (vs. +13% MoM/
+60% YoY in September 2026), to ¥204bn.
• Domestic orders were -18% MoM/+47% YoY (vs. +24% MoM/+46% YoY in
September), at ¥52bn.
• Overseas orders were -5% MoM/+41% YoY (vs. +9% MoM/+67% YoY  in
September), at ¥152bn.
Our view:
• For overseas orders, the growth remains solid on a YoY basis. We think the
sequential decline is likely from China as its manufacturing PMI edged down

to 49.3 in Oct (vs. 50.1 in Sep). Meanwhile, North America and Europe could
prove more resilient, with manufacturing PMIs staying relatively firm at
55.5/52.7 in Oct (vs. 54.5/52.9 in Sep)
• We remain OW on both Airtac (1590.TW) and Hiwin (2049.TW), as we see
company-specific drivers on top of the industry recovery trend:
° Airtac should continue to benefit from its own market share gains.
Valuation also appears undemanding at 15x 2027e P/E (vs. 25x average

since 2020).
° Hiwin should  see margin expansion in the coming quarters, driven by
higher utilization and price hikes. Current valuation of 27x 2027 P/E
implies upside vs. peak cycle valuation at 35-40x P/E.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 25x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). We think our valuation of 25x P/E is reasonable (vs. average of 25x since
2020).
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China
n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation

Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple
of 34x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 50% operating profit

CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup
n Market share gains
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand
n More pricing pressure
n Market share loss
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

雙鴻(3324)：3Q26 財報不如預期，營運逐季向上 
建議：維持買進

一、雙鴻為散熱解決方案供應商  雙鴻成立於 1998 年 8 月，為國內前二大 NB 散熱模組廠，過去主要生產散熱
器、散熱片、散熱模組、散熱導管、風扇等，現在已轉型為提供客戶完整散熱解
決方案供應商。主要應用於筆記型電腦、桌上型電腦、智慧型手機及伺服器等領
域。3Q26 產品應用領域 Server 81%、PC 14%、VGA/Gaming 4%、及 Others(手
機及車用) 1%。另外 3Q26 水冷業務比重 62%(1H26 約為 55~60%)。

二、3Q26 毛利率 25.65%，低於
預期，3Q26 EPS 11.59 元 
 雙鴻 3Q26 EPS 11.59 元，低於台新預估的 14.13 元，獲利達成率 82%。本業
來看，3Q26 營收 97.87 億元，+12.5%QOQ，+64.3%YOY，低於市場預期之
+15~20%QOQ，七月營收有遞延認列，實際拉貨力道未如預期顯著，3Q26 毛
利率 25.65%，較 2Q26 再衰退 2.77 個百分點，亦低於台新預估的 30%，主要
是報廢品打消，預期 4Q26 毛利率回升，3Q26 EPS 11.59 元。雙鴻 10 月營收
33.72 億元，+17%MOM，+45%YOY，獲利達成率 31%，符合預期，預期 11 月
營收及 12 月營收將優於 10 月，預估 4Q26 營收 110.37 億元，+12.8%QOQ，
+45.6%YOY，預估 4Q26 毛利率 28%，預估 4Q26 EPS 14.79 元。

三、營運逐季成長，氣冷及水冷
兩塊業務均樂觀 
 2025 年雙鴻水冷營收比重 35%，1Q26 提升至 55%，3Q26 提升至 62%，預期
2027 年將提升至 80%，帶動公司業績大幅增長。氣冷部分，2024 年月營收 2-
2.5 億元，2025 年 4～4.5 億元，2026 年平均 6～7 億元，氣冷水冷均樂觀，以
AWS TR3 來看，氣冷及水冷比重至少是 70~80%對比 20~30%，TR4 將為 100%
全水冷，水冷大趨勢不變，VR 9 月起量，VR 營收逐季增，TR3 9 月起量 Compute 
tray 及 Switch tray 及 Rack manifolds 都有相關產品出貨，預計 12 月單月 ASIC
營收比重有機會來到 15~25%，ASIC 水冷產值高於GPU 客戶，其中 Mata>AWS，
2027 年 ASIC 增長可期，公司預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU。2026 年可
視為 CDU 元年，2027 年營收跳增可期。台新預估 4Q26 營收 110.37 億元，

江誼蓁 
yizhen0808@yuanta.com

3Q26 EPS 低於市場預期 14%，預期 4Q26 毛利率小幅回升 
嘉澤 3Q26 營收 99.58 億元，季增 4.1%/年增 18.2%，其中伺服器營收年增
55%，惟記憶體與 PCB 缺料壓抑 CPU Socket 拉貨，原物料成本維持高檔，
加上新平台導入慢於預期，毛利率季減 0.6 個百分點至 44.8%。薪資費用高
於預期以及業外淨收益亦較上季下降，EPS 為 17.06 元，低於本中心/市場
預期 11.0%/14.4%。考量缺料延續及新平台挹注有限，下修 4Q26 營收
5.8%至 103.89 億元，季增 4.3%/年增 12.2%，毛利率估季增 0.3 個百分點
至 45.1%，EPS 下修 12.6%至 18.79 元。 
QD 擴產及伺服器平台換代帶動 2027 年營收年增 30% 
QD 產能滿載，公司目標於未來一季將產能倍增，預估 QD 占總營收比重於
2026 年 12 月達 3–5%，2027 年全年接近 10%。AMD Venice 預計自 1Q27
末加速放量，2027 年平均採用率約 20%。Intel Oak Stream 預計於 1Q27
末至 2Q27 初導入，2H27 採用率估達 10–15%。新平台有助提升 CPU 
Socket 單價及 PCIe 6.0 連接器採用率，預估 2027 年伺服器營收年增 50%，
帶動整體營收年增 30%。 
維持買進及目標價 2,200 元 
伺服器缺料及 PC 業務疲弱使營收低於前估，原物料成本居高、新平台滲透
慢於預期，加上低於公司平均毛利率的 QD 占比提高及薪資費用增加，下修
2026/2027 年 EPS 6.0%/5.4%至 74.92/106.29 元。評價方面，本中心仍看
好 2027 年 QD 擴產將擴大營收與獲利貢獻，Venice 與 Oak Stream 逐步放
量則有助改善 CPU Socket 產品組合。隨營收規模擴大，預估 2027 年營益
率由 24.5%回升至 28.3%，帶動 EPS 年增 41.9%，因此維持買進評等，以 
2027 年預估 EPS 106.29 元 21 倍本益比推得目標價 2,200 元。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
3Q26 EPS 為 17.06 元，低於市場預期 14%，主因薪資費用提升以及
原物料成本居高、新平台導入進度不如預期，毛利率季減至 44.8%。 
⧫  
記憶體與 PCB 缺料持續壓抑 CPU Socket 拉貨，下修 4Q26 EPS 
12.6%至 18.79 元，預期毛利率小幅回升。 
⧫  
QD 放量與 AMD/Intel 新平台導入帶動 2027 年營收年增 30%、EPS
年增 42%至 106.29 元，維持買進及目標價 2,200 元。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電子零組件 2026 年 11 月 16 日 Lotes 第2 頁，共 16頁
營運分析
3Q26 毛利率低於預期，薪資費用提升，EPS 低於市場預期 14%
嘉澤 3Q26 營收 99.58 億元，季增 4.1%/年增 18.2%，低於本中心/市場預期 1.7%/2.5%，其中伺服
器營收年增 55%，占總營收 53.1%，惟記憶體與 PCB 缺料持續限制客戶出貨，CPU Socket 拉貨
低於預期。原物料成本維持高檔，伺服器新平台導入慢於預期，加上部分桌機客戶因缺料改採支援
DDR4 或 DDR3 的舊平台，使低單價、低毛利產品占比偏高，毛利率季減 0.6 個百分點至 44.8%，
低於本中心/市場預期 0.7/0.9 個百分點。中國廠區自 2Q26 起調薪，使費用率年增約 0.7 個百分點。
受惠於營收規模擴大，費用率仍季減 1.6 個百分點至 21.9%，營益率升至 22.9%，營業利益季增
8.6%至 22.84 億元。業外淨收益由上季 4.95 億元降至 0.95 億元，歸屬母公司淨利季減 3.3%至

19.19 億元。EPS 為 17.06 元，季減 3.5%/年減 17.6%，低於本中心/市場預期 11.0%/14.4%。

新平台導入速度不如預期，下修 4Q26 EPS 13%至 18.79 元
嘉澤 10 月營收 32.0 億元，月減 5%/年增 12%，達本中心 4Q26 營收原估的 29%。公司預期 4Q26
營收呈低個位數季增，零組件缺料仍是主要出貨限制，因此本中心下修 4Q26 營收 5.8%至 103.89
億元，季增 4.3%/年增 12.2%。SOCAMM 等新品出貨增加有助毛利率回升，惟 AMD Venice 年底
採用率僅估 3–5%，Intel Oak Stream 預計於 1Q27 末至 2Q27 初開始導入，高針腳數 CPU Socket

對 4Q26 產品組合的挹注低於前估，加上原物料成本維持高檔，以及毛利率低於公司平均的 QD 出
貨增加，本中心下修 4Q26 毛利率 1.4 個百分點至 45.1%，季增 0.3 個百分點。預估營業利益季增
10.2%至 25.17 億元，EPS 下修 12.6%至 18.79 元，季增約 10%/年減 16.5%。

QD 擴產與伺服器平台換代帶動 2027 年營收年增 30%
本中心預估嘉澤 2027 年營收年增 30%，主要成長動能來自 QD 放量與通用伺服器平台換代。公司
預估 2027 年伺服器整機出貨量年增 10–15%，新平台導入也將推升 CPU Socket 單價，並帶動
PCIe 6.0 連接器採用率提升。AMD Venice 目前採用率不到 3%，預計自 1Q27 末加速放量，2027
年全年採用率約 20%。Intel Oak Stream 預計於 1Q27 末至 2Q27 初開始導入，2H27 採用率估達

10–15%，營收貢獻主要落在下半年。隨 PCIe 連接器與線材市占率提升，本中心預估 2026/2027
年伺服器營收分別年增 56%/50%。嘉澤另預計於 2H27 開始出貨 Arm CPU 平台的客製化 Socket。
QD 目前產能滿載，公司目標於未來一季將產能倍增。預估 QD 占總營收比重於 2026 年 12 月達
3–5%，2027 年全年接近 10%。QD 放量將稀釋整體毛利率，但新增營業費用相對有限，規模擴大
後仍可挹注營業利益及 EPS。隨伺服器新平台採用率提高及 SOCAMM 出貨增加，本中心預估
2027 年毛利率由 46.2%回升至 48.5%。NPO Socket 預計於 2027 年以總頻寬 3.2Tbps 的產品小量
出貨，占營收不到 2%，2028 年隨單價較高的 6.4Tbps 產品導入，營收貢獻將更為明顯。
非伺服器業務方面，公司預估 2027 年 PC 市場出貨量再減約 10%。本中心估嘉澤 2026/2027 年筆
電營收分別年減 17%/4%，桌機營收年減 30%/年增 1%;策略客戶營收年增 3%/2%，車用年增

27%/15%，工業及醫療年增 22%/20%。嘉基受惠於高速線材出貨成長，營收預估年增 12%/40%。

產業概況  
2026 年 PC 出貨恐衰退近雙位數 
記憶體價格大漲，影響 PC 出貨。受到上游記憶體成本大幅上揚，PC 廠商即使轉嫁或降低規格，
均將影響消費者購買意願，因此預估 2026 年 PC(NB+DT)出貨衰退 9.6%，NB 出貨亦年減 9.7%。
2027 年 PC 及 NB 出貨量才有機會回升低個位數。 
上半年消費者提前拉貨，下半年出貨待觀察。1H26 市場預期記憶體及儲存裝置未來大漲，因此客
戶有提前拉貨現象，IDC 統計全球 PC 出貨 679 萬台，季減 16%，但年增 3%，2Q26 起 PC 步入
衰退 5%，下半年更衰退至 15~20%。

### Page 6

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電子零組件 2026 年 11 月 16 日 Lotes 第6 頁，共 16頁  
AI、ASIC、通用型強勢出擊，2026/2027 年美系 CSP 資本支出估年增 95%/46%  
在 ChatGPT 等 AI 應用陸續推向市場，各大業者紛紛開始研發自有超級大模型，包括 Gemini 
(Google)、Claude (Anthropic)、Llama (Meta) 等，各大品牌開始大舉投入大型語言模型(LLM)市場，
且每一個產品的智慧皆持續提高，部分消費者也陸續從 ChatGPT 陣營轉移至 Gemini 陣營，反映
出消費者開始逐漸提升對 AI 的黏著度。根據 Grand View Research 調查，2024 年全球 AI 市場營
收約 2,792 億美元，預估 2030 年市場規模超過 3 兆美元，擁有超過 30% 的年複合增長率。 
面對這爆發性的長期成長趨勢，CSP 業者(Amazon、Microsoft、Google、Meta)在法說會上一致強
調，AI 投資已轉變為一項橫跨 2026 年的結構性戰略，CSP 大廠不僅持續擴大對 GB300 等次世代
AI 伺服器的採購，更將重點放在資料中心基礎設施的長期戰略規劃，以應對 AI 運算對電力及散熱
系統強勁成長的需求，確保未來數年內擁有足夠的能源與容量來支持持續迭代的大模型部署。本中
心認為，2026 年美國主要 CSP 資本支出將年增 95%，且將有一半的成長來自於資料中心建置，代
表了資料中心的不足及未來伺服器需求。明年不僅僅是 NVIDIA 的天下，AWS、Google、Meta 
ASIC 的崛起也將是一大關鍵，其中又以 Google TPU 需求最為強勁，已經有兩個客戶預計將採購
TPU。 
另外，受惠於 AI 訓練與推論需求爆發的連帶效應，2026/2027 年通用型伺服器預估出貨量將年增
10%/9%，其中以存儲型伺服器需求最為強勁。隨著生成式 AI 應用普及和使用者數量暴增，CSP
等業者必須不斷擴充容量，且不僅限於高階 GPU 伺服器，龐大的 AI 模型訓練和推論在使用狀態中
將消耗大量的數據(DATA)，使得存儲型伺服器的需求大增。

### Page 7

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電子零組件 2026 年 11 月 16 日 Lotes 第7 頁，共 16頁  
ASIC 進入高速成長期，GPU 需求仍維持擴張 
從 AI 晶片預期出貨來看，GPU 與 ASIC 需求均持續成長，但 2026 年後 ASIC 成長動能明顯加速。
Nvidia GPU 出貨量預估由 2025 年 565.7 萬顆增至 2026 年 650 萬顆、2027 年 850 萬顆，年增
15%及 31%；AMD 亦由 2025 年 90 萬顆增至 2027 年 180 萬顆，顯示通用 GPU 算力需求仍維持
成長。 
相較之下，ASIC 受惠 CSP 加速自研晶片及導入規模擴大，總出貨量預估由 2025 年 380 萬顆大幅
增至 2026 年 725 萬顆、2027 年 1,360 萬顆，年增率達 91%及 88%，成長速度明顯高於 GPU。其
中 Google 仍為最大 ASIC 使用者，2027 年用量預估達 800 萬顆，AWS 亦增至 320 萬顆，
Microsoft 及 Meta 同步擴大部署。我們認為，在資料中心電力逐漸成為算力擴張瓶頸下，CSP 將
更重視每瓦運算效能及整體運算成本，ASIC 針對特定運算工作的效率優勢有望推升滲透率，惟
GPU 憑藉通用性及軟體生態系優勢，仍將維持重要地位。

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：電子零組件 2026 年 11 月 16 日 Lotes 第8 頁，共 16頁
獲利調整與股票評價
下修 2026/2027 年 EPS 6.0%/5.4%，維持買進及目標價 2,200 元
伺 服 器 供 應 鏈 缺 料 持 續 壓 抑 CPU Socket 拉 貨 ， 筆 電 及 桌 機 營 收 亦 低 於 前 估 ， 本 中 心 下 修
2026/2027 年營收 2.0%/2.6%至 392.43/510.58 億元，分別年增 16.2%/30.1%。原物料成本維持高
檔，AMD 及 Intel 新平台採用率提升慢於預期，加上 QD 營收占比提高，使毛利率改善幅度低於前
估。本中心下修 2026/2027 年毛利率 0.5/0.6 個百分點至 46.2%/48.5%。費用方面，中國廠區調薪
推升固定薪資支出，公司預估 2027 年單季營業費用約 22–25 億元。考量薪資費用高於前估，下修
2026/2027 年 EPS 6.0%/5.4%至 74.92/106.29 元，分別年增 6.8%/41.9%。

評價方面，本中心仍看好 2027 年 QD 擴產將擴大營收與獲利貢獻，Venice 與 Oak Stream 逐步放
量則有助改善 CPU Socket 產品組合。隨營收規模擴大，預估 2027 年營益率由 24.5%回升至
28.3%，帶動 EPS 年增 41.9%，因此維持買進評等，以 2027 年預估 EPS 106.29 元 21 倍本益比
推得目標價 2,200 元。

公司簡介  
嘉澤成立於 1986 年，專門生產及設計 PCIe、DDR、SATA 等連接器及 USB 連接埠，公司擁有完整的生產流程，包含設計、
加工、成型、沖壓、電鍍及自動化組裝，可提供客製化需求及標準型產品。公司為全球前三大 Server Socket (CPU 插槽)供
應商，市佔率約為 30-35%，同業為鴻海集團(鴻騰精密)及安費諾等。該產業的進入門檻高，多數產品皆已申請專利，且公
司實驗室已通過 ISO 認證，可縮短測試時間，並降低測試費用。嘉澤產品為電腦、伺服器、消費性電子產品之各式連接器
及零組件，旗下有四大事業體，分別是 PC/NB、伺服器、子公司嘉基及其他業務(包括 Type-C 連接器)。

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分： 嘉澤整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於低風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排名亦排行於中間偏前的
位置，在電子零組件行業中的公司中排名略領先於同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險： 嘉澤的整體曝險屬於中等水準，優於電子零組件行業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的 ESG 議
題包含公司治理、商業道德與資源使用等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：嘉澤在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力屬中等。公司遵循適當的 ESG 揭露，
表明對投資者和公眾的強烈責任感。其 ESG 相關問題由董事會或經營團隊負責，建議將這些整合到核心業務戰略中。

分析師聲明 
主要負責撰寫本研究報告全文或部分內容之分析師，茲針對本報告所載證券或證券發行機構，於此聲明：(1) 文中所述觀點皆準確反映其個人對各證券或
證券發行機構之看法；(2) 研究部分析師於本研究報告中所提出之特定投資建議或觀點，與其過去、現在、未來薪酬的任何部份皆無直接或間接關聯。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

註：元大給予個股之目標價係依 12 個月投資期間計算。大中華探索系列報告並無正式之 12 個月目標價，其投資建議乃根據分析師報告中之指定期間分析
而得。

設備、製造、研發、創新、設計等完整服務
的企業。3Q26 產品營收比重，氟素內襯設
備營收占 69.7% ， 氟 素 內 襯 管 件 類 占
10.2%，氟素應用材料類占 16.5 %，氟素
工程收入占 3.7%。3Q26 公司營收按地區
別區分，台灣占 47.4%，中國占 48.2%，美
國占 0.5%，其他亞洲占 1.6%，歐洲占

主要客戶： 
主要競爭對手：

葉家宏 bill.yeh@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

上品 (4770 TT) 
在手訂單金額提升，持續擴充產能因應

永豐觀點 
上品在手訂單金額提升至 80 億元，隨著先前低毛利訂單陸續消化完畢，產品組合
預期改善，公司規劃於 2027 持續進行產能擴充，後市展望持續正向，研究部維持
正向看待上品獲利表現。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議維持買進，主要係因：上品在手訂單金額持續提升至 80 億元，反映台灣
及中國地區接單表現持續暢旺，隨著公司低毛利訂單出貨完畢，產品組合應逐步好
轉，且公司規劃於 2027 持續擴充產能，挹注未來營收動能，研究部預估公司
2026/2027 稅後 EPS 14.55/23.01 元，參考歷史 PER 區間約位於 10-20X，以
2027 年預估 EPS，給予 15X PER 評價，目標價 345 元，投資建議維持買進。

ESG 評析 
上品企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 B+等。

### Page 2

2

上品為從事氟素樹脂材料相關製品之生產廠商：公司成立於 1981 年，主要經營氟
素樹脂材料（鐵氟龍）相關製品，包括氟素原料加工之半成品及成品生產及銷售，
下游應用廣泛，是全球氟素樹脂加工應用廠商中少數能同時滿足各階段設備、製
造、研發、創新、設計等完整服務的企業。3Q26 產品營收比重，氟素內襯設備營
收占 69.7%，氟素內襯管件類占 10.2%，氟素應用材料類占 16.5 %，氟素工程收
入占 3.7%；3Q26 公司產品依應用類別區分，半導體占營收 95.1%，化工應用占
比為 3.1%，面板及太陽能應用占 1.4%，其他應用占 0.4%，3Q26 公司營收按地
區別區分，台灣占 47.4%，中國占 48.2%，美國占 0.5%，其他亞洲占 1.6%，歐
洲占 2.2%。 
3Q26 EPS 4.27 元，優於預期：上品 3Q26 營收 16 億元（+22.5% QoQ，+14.4% 
YoY），優於預期，主因上品於 2Q26 擴產，並持續拉升產能利用率，推升營收表
現，毛利率 34.64%（-1.91pts. QoQ，+4.91pts. YoY），低於預期，主因上品出
貨較多低毛利訂單，影響產品組合所致，稅後淨利 34.1 億元（+37.2% QoQ，
+39.9% YoY），優於預期，其中，營業費用低於預期，主因認列預期信用損失金
額降低所致，3Q26 稅後 EPS 4.27 元，優於預期。

### Page 3

在手訂單金額持續提升：上品在手訂單金額達 80 億元，較前次發報告時 75 億元
持續提升，其中，約 75%為彰濱廠訂單，約 25%為嘉興廠訂單，預期到年底在手
訂單金額將持續再提升。2026 接單槽車數量達 1,100 台，反映台灣化學品廠因應
晶圓廠大客戶需求而擴充產能，對於上品內襯設備及內襯管件需求同步增溫，而中
國記憶體廠及化學品廠亦持續擴充，下單動能有所增溫。隨著市況於 2026 大幅改
善，彰濱廠及嘉興廠已可挑選毛利率較佳的訂單進行承接，產品組合預期將逐步好
轉，有利其毛利率逐步回升。 
2027 上品計畫持續擴充產能：因應客戶需求強勁，公司於 2Q26 進行擴產，並持
續招工補充人力，拉升產能利用率，目前彰濱廠及嘉興廠已達近滿載水準，公司年
初彰濱廠槽車約當產能約為月產能 80 台，近期已提升至月產能 130 台，預期年底
將達滿載規模月產能 140 台，公司規劃將於 6M27 再擴充至月產能 180 台，嘉興
廠亦規劃提升產能約 30%，以因應客戶強勁需求。而先前承接的低毛利訂單仍有
部分尚未出貨完畢，預期將於 1Q27 出貨完畢，因營收比重持續下降，預期產品組
合應持續改善，有利毛利率及獲利表現回升。

後市展望正向，投資建議維持買進：展望後市，台灣化學品廠及中國記憶體、化學
品廠擴建，推升對上品氟素樹脂產品需求，上品在手訂單金額持續提升，隨著上品
先前承接低毛利訂單陸續出貨完畢，產品組合應逐步改善，推升毛利率及獲利表現
回升。上品規劃於 2027 持續進行產能擴充，挹注未來營收動能，且已可挑選毛利
率較佳的訂單進行承接，後市獲利展望正向。研究部預估公司 2026 營收 56.9 億
元（+20.3% YoY），毛利率 35.1%，稅後淨利 11.6 億元（+41.6% YoY），稅後
EPS 14.55 元，預估 2027 營收 76.8 億元（+35.1% YoY），毛利率 38.31%，稅
後淨利 18.4 億元（+59.2% YoY），稅後 EPS 23.01 元，參考歷史 PER 區間約位
於 10-20X，以 2027 年預估 EPS，給予 15X PER 評價，目標價 345 元，維持買進
投資建議。

### Page 1

2026년 10월 29일 
기업분석

SK하이닉스 (000660) 
높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클

Buy(유지) 
목표주가 3,150,000원, 현재 주가(10/28): 1,487,000원 
▶ Analyst 박준영 jyp94@hanwha.com 3772-7481 / RA 김나우 now.kim@hanwha.com 3772-7710

3Q26 Review: HBM4 출하 확대와 예상보다 강한 가격 상승 
분기 매출액 104.6조원(QoQ +32%, YoY +328%), 영업이익 83.3조원(QoQ +38%, YoY +632%)을 기록하며 시장 기대
치를 상회함. 영업이익률은 전분기 대비 약 3%p 상승한 80%로 사상 최대 분기 실적을 달성함. 특히 DRAM ASP가 전
분기 대비 20% 후반 상승하며 당사 기존 예상치인 +23%를 상회한 점에 주목할 필요가 있음. NAND ASP 역시 20% 
중반 상승하며 예상보다 강한 가격 흐름을 나타냄. DRAM ASP 상승에는 기존 DRAM의 가격 인상과 함께 3분기부터 
본격화된 HBM4 양산 및 출하 확대에 따른 제품 믹스 개선 효과가 반영됨. 서버 DDR5 판매량 역시 전분기 대비 40% 
증가하며 실적 성장을 뒷받침함. 특히 HBM4는 기존 HBM3E 대비 높은 기술적 난도를 반영한 가격으로 공급되고 있어 
향후 출하 비중 확대에 따른 추가적인 ASP 상승 효과도 기대됨. 
이번 실적에서 중요한 부분은 예상보다 높은 가격 상승폭이 2027년 실적 추정치의 상향 가능성을 높이고 있다는 점임. 3
분기 DRAM ASP가 기존 추정치를 상회한 가운데 4분기에도 HBM4 출하 확대와 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 이어질 
것으로 예상되기 때문임. 동사는 4분기 DRAM 출하량이 전분기 대비 20% 중반 증가할 것으로 전망했으며, HBM4 출
하량이 HBM3E를 넘어 주력 제품으로 자리 잡을 것으로 예상함. 특히 HBM4의 양산 수율이 당초 목표를 상회하고 있
으며 높은 고객 수요에 대응하기 위해 생산성 극대화에 집중하고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. HBM4 출하 확대에 따른 믹스 
개선과 기존 DRAM 가격 상승이 동시에 진행되는 가운데 HBM과 일반 DRAM의 수익성 격차 역시 유의미하게 축소
될 것으로 예상됨. 이는 특정 제품에 대한 수익성 의존도가 낮아지면서 DRAM 사업 전반의 이익 체력이 강화되고 있음
을 의미함. 메모리 산업의 높은 영업 레버리지를 고려하면 예상보다 높은 ASP와 출하량 증가는 2027년 이익 추정치의 
추가 상향으로 연결될 가능성이 높다고 판단함.

공급 부족의 장기화와 LTA 확대, 메모리 산업의 이익 구조 변화 
이번 컨퍼런스콜에서 확인된 또 다른 중요한 변화는 공급 부족의 장기화와 장기공급계약(LTA) 확대임. 동사는 첨단 공정
의 기술적 난도 상승과 신규 생산시설 구축에 소요되는 시간을 고려할 때 메모리 공급이 수요를 충족하지 못하는 환경이 
2030년까지 지속될 것으로 전망함. 특히 글로벌 주요 고객사 14곳과 장기공급계약을 체결했으며, 2027~2028년 전체 생
산능력의 50% 이상에 해당하는 물량을 확보함. HBM의 경우 2029년까지의 물량이 이미 판매 확정된 상황으로 중장기 
수요 가시성이 상당히 높아졌다고 판단함. 주목할 부분은 LTA가 단순한 공급물량 확보를 넘어 메모리 업체의 사업 구조
와 이익 안정성을 변화시키고 있다는 점임. 계약 가격은 고객 및 제품별로 차이가 있으나 합의된 범위 내에서 시장 상황
을 반영하는 구조로 운영되고 있으며 예치금 등 실질적인 계약 이행력을 높이는 장치도 포함됨. 이는 장기적인 판매물량
을 확보하면서도 업황 상승에 따른 가격 인상 효과를 일정 부분 누릴 수 있다는 점에서 긍정적임.

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

2 
특히 전체 생산능력의 50% 이상을 LTA로 확보하면서 나머지 물량은 시장 상황에 따라 탄력적으로 대응할 수 있다는 점
에 주목할 필요가 있음. 장기계약을 통해 안정적인 매출 기반을 확보하는 동시에 미계약 물량을 활용해 추가적인 가격 
상승에 대응할 수 있기 때문임. 물론 장기계약이 메모리 가격 하락을 완전히 방어하는 것은 아니지만, 과거와 비교해 물
량 가시성과 고객사와의 협상력이 동시에 개선되고 있다는 점은 분명함. 과거 메모리 산업이 높은 이익을 창출하는 국면
에서도 낮은 밸류에이션을 적용 받았던 이유 중 하나는 이익의 지속성에 대한 불확실성이었음. 그러나 장기계약 확대와 
공급 제약의 장기화가 동시에 진행된다면 시장이 평가하는 이익의 지속성 역시 높아질 수 있음. 따라서 향후 주가 상승
은 단순한 EPS 추정치 상향뿐 아니라 이익 변동성 축소에 따른 밸류에이션 재평가가 동시에 진행되는 형태로 나타날 가
능성이 높다고 판단함. 
AI 인프라 투자에 대한 우려 역시 중장기 메모리 수요를 훼손할 가능성은 제한적이라고 판단함. 동사는 주요 하이퍼스케
일러의 클라우드 매출이 전년 대비 30~80% 성장하고 있으며 기업용 AI 솔루션 계약 확대와 클라우드 수주잔고 증가를 
통해 AI 투자의 사업적 성과가 확인되고 있다고 설명함. 특히 초기 AI 투자가 소수 빅테크에 집중되었던 것과 달리 최근
에는 일반기업의 자체 인프라 구축, 소버린 AI, 온프레미스 등으로 투자 주체가 다변화되고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. 특정 
사업자의 투자 속도 조절이 전체 AI 인프라 수요에 미치는 영향은 과거 대비 완화될 수 있기 때문임. AI 투자에 대한 우
려가 지속되는 상황에서도 실제 메모리 수요와 고객사들의 장기계약 확대가 확인되고 있다는 점에서 메모리 업황의 구조
적 성장에 대한 긍정적인 시각을 유지함.

HBM4 경쟁력 강화와 CAPEX 확대, 이익 성장과 주주환원의 선순환 
HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대와 신규 생산능력 확보 역시 2027년 실적 성장의 중요한 기반이 될 전망임. 동사는 M15X
의 생산능력을 연말까지 최대 수준으로 확대할 계획이며 HBM4의 양산 수율 역시 당초 목표를 상회하는 등 안정적인 
생산체계를 확보하고 있음. 특히 HBM 수요가 공급능력의 두 배를 상회하는 상황에서 고객사들의 높은 선호가 이어지고 
있다는 점은 경쟁력 유지에 긍정적임. 최근 일부 고객사들이 HBM 적층 단수를 낮추는 방안을 검토하고 있으나 이는 공
급 부족 상황에서 제한된 물량을 효율적으로 활용하기 위한 전략으로 판단됨. 적층 단수 조정이 전체 HBM 수요 감소로 
직결되는 것은 아니며 오히려 후공정 생산능력에 대한 요구가 확대될 가능성도 존재함. HBM4E와 커스텀 HBM 개발 
역시 고객사 일정에 맞춰 진행되고 있어 차세대 제품에서도 경쟁력을 유지할 것으로 기대됨. 
한편 2027년 CAPEX는 선단 공정 전환과 용인 클러스터를 비롯한 국내외 생산 인프라 확충으로 전년 대비 증가할 전망
임. 다만 CAPEX 확대를 과거와 같은 공급 과잉의 신호로 해석할 필요는 없다고 판단함. 선단 공정의 기술적 난도 상승
과 고부가 제품 비중 확대에 따라 투자금액 증가가 실제 생산량 증가로 정비례하지 않기 때문임. 특히 동사는 신규 팹 
건설 일정을 앞당기면서도 클린룸은 순차적으로 개방하고 실제 생산능력 확대는 최종 수요와 수익성을 고려해 단계적으
로 진행할 계획임. 고객사의 투자 계획뿐 아니라 재무건전성과 자금조달 여력, 공급망 준비 상황까지 점검해 CAPEX 집
행에 반영한다는 점도 긍정적임. 이는 AI 인프라 투자 확대에 대응하면서도 과거 메모리 산업의 공급 과잉 위험을 최소
화하기 위한 전략으로 판단함. 
동사는 이에 더해 2026년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원 규모의 주주환원 계획을 발표했음. 현금배당 30조원 이상, 자사주 
매입 및 소각 60조원 이상으로 구성되며, 기존에 약속했던 잉여현금흐름(FCF)의 50% 이상을 주주에게 환원한다는 원칙
을 구체화한 것임. 지난 8월 발표한 40조원 규모의 자사주 매입은 10월 21일 완료됐으며, 해당 주식은 향후 소각될 예정
임. 이번 3분기 이사회에서는 현금배당 14조원과 추가 자사주 매입 20조원을 결의했으며, 자사주 매입액 중 10조원은 전
량 소각하고 나머지 10조원은 임직원 보상에 활용할 계획임. 임직원 보상용 자사주는 주주환원 규모에서 제외됨. 특히 
향후에도 자사주 매입을 지속할 계획이라는 점에서 주주환원 정책의 지속성이 강화됐다는 판단임.

### Page 3

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

3 
3분기 주당 배당금은 2만원으로, 실적 개선을 반영한 일반배당 1만원과 키옥시아 투자수익을 활용한 특별배당 1만원으
로 구성됨. 총 배당 규모는 약 14조원이며 회사는 올해 실현한 키옥시아 투자수익의 대부분을 3분기 및 연말 특별배당을 
통해 주주에게 환원할 계획임. 이번 결정은 메모리 가격 상승과 고부가 제품 확대에 따른 현금창출력 개선이 실질적인 
주주환원 확대로 연결되고 있음을 보여줌. 특히 2027년 설비투자 확대가 예상되는 상황에서도 대규모 현금배당과 자사
주 소각을 병행한다는 점은 재무적 여력에 대한 자신감을 반영하는 것으로 판단됨. 향후에도 안정적인 실적 성장과 FCF 
확대가 이어질 경우 주주환원의 지속 가능성이 높아질 전망이며 이는 과거 대비 높아진 이익 창출력과 함께 중장기 밸류
에이션 재평가를 뒷받침하는 요인으로 작용할 것으로 예상됨. 
결국 이번 실적발표에서 확인된 핵심은 예상보다 강한 메모리 가격 상승과 HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대, 그리고 장기계
약을 통한 이익 가시성 개선임. 특히 HBM4의 높은 가격과 안정적인 양산 수율, 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 동시에 확
인되면서 2027년 이익 추정치의 추가 상향 가능성이 높아졌다고 판단함. 여기에 LTA 확대에 따른 중장기 이익 안정성 
개선과 대규모 주주환원까지 더해지고 있음. 실적 추정치 상향과 이익의 지속성 개선이 동시에 진행되고 있다는 점에서 
긍정적인 투자 시각을 유지함.

전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 5

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

5 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 09 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계 
금융투자상품의 비중 96.4% 3.6% 0.0% 100.0%

2026.11.04   
LG에너지솔루션 
(373220)

ESS 수주 모멘텀 이어진다 Company Brief

3Q26 실적, 원-달러 환율 하락에도 시장 기대치 대폭 상회 
동사의 3Q26 실적은 매출 9.6조원(+59% YoY, +28% QoQ), 영업이익 7,560억원 
(+26% YoY, +568% QoQ)으로 원/달러 환율 하락에도 불구하고 시장기대치(매출 
8.8조원, 영업이익 3,217억원)를 크게 상회했다. 당사는 Stellantis와의 캐나다 
합작관계 종료에 따른 보상금 등을 포함해 약 4,250억원의 일회성 이익이 반영된 
것으로 추정한다. 이를 제외한 영업이익은 약 3,310억원으로, 시장기대치에 대체로 
부합한 것으로 판단된다. AMPC는 미국 ESS 생산량 증가와 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동에 
힘입어 전분기 대비 약 73% 증가한 4,169억원을 기록했다. 사업부문별로는 ESS의 
실적 개선이 두드러졌다. 상반기 배터리 패키징 병목으로 이연됐던 물량 출하가 
집중되면서 ESS 출하량은 전분기 대비 60% 가량 증가한 것으로 추정된다. 자동차 
전지도 유럽향 물량 회복과 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동으로 매출이 전분기 대비 약 
33%로 증가했으며, 고정비 부담 완화에 따라 적자폭도 뚜렷하게 축소된 것으로 
판단된다. 소형전지는 주요 고객사인 Tesla의 판매 호조에 따른 출하 증가로 매출 
회복과 수익성을 뒷받침한 것으로 보인다. 
4Q26 ESS 중심의 수익성 개선과 수주 모멘텀 지속될 전망 
미국 ESS 시장은 신재생 에너지 확대와 AI 데이터센터의 전력 수요 증가에 힘입어 
빠르게 성장하고 있다. 특히 정책 환경도 비PFE 공급망을 확보한 업체에 유리하게 
작용하고 있다. 동사는 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스 콜에서 ESS 수주잔고가 약 
160GWh이며, 2028년까지 캐파의 대부분에 대응하는 물량을 확보했다고 밝혔다. 
또한 현재 다수의 고객사들과 2028년 이후 공급 물량을 협의하고 있어 연말까지 
추가적인 수주 모멘텀이 이어질 것으로 예상된다. 이를 바탕으로 4Q26에도 동사의 
본업 실적은 ESS 중심의 개선세를 이어갈 전망이다. 전분기에 반영된 대규모 일회성 
이익의 기저효과로 영업이익은 감소하겠지만, 이를 제외하면 출하량 증가와 가동률 
상승에 따른 점진적인 수익성 개선이 예상된다. 수익성 중심의 사업 효율화를 위해 
소형 IT용 파우치 사업을 축소하고 있다는 점도 중장기 손익 개선에 긍정적이다. 이를 
반영한 4Q26 매출과 영업이익은 각각 약 10.5조원(+63% YoY, +9% QoQ), 
4,150억원(흑자전환 YoY, -45% QoQ)으로 추정된다. 
매수 투자의견과 목표주가 유지 
동사에 대한 매수 투자의견과 목표주가를 유지한다. 북미 ESS 수요 증가와 공급망 
재편에 따른 신규 수주 확대가 중장기 실적 성장을 뒷받침할 전망이다. 특히 ESS용 
생산라인 전환과 가동률 상승이 본격화되면 매출 증가에 더해 고정비 부담 완화와 
AMPC 수혜 확대가 맞물리면서 수익성도 개선될 것으로 예상된다. ESS의 역할이 
신재생 에너지 연계용 저장장치를 넘어 AI 데이터센터의 안정적인 전력 공급을 
지원하는 핵심 인프라로 확대되고 있다는 점에도 주목할 필요가 있다. 이에 따라 
동사의 밸류에이션 역시 기존 EV 중심의 평가에서 벗어나 전력 인프라 산업의 
성장성을 반영하는 방향으로 재평가되어야 한다. 업종 내 실적 개선 가시성과 정책 
수혜가 뚜렷하고, 4Q26 신규 수주에 따른 주가 상승 가능성도 높다는 점에서 동사에 
대한 비중 확대 전략이 유효하다는 판단이다. 
Buy (Maintain)

당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

종목추천 투자등급

 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상

Document 337: How Polish households are saving and investing in 2026

Source PDF: all report pdf/How Polish households are saving and investing in 2026.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-16
Pages: 11
Status: ok

### Page 1

Why Poland’s new Personal Investment Account could prove
popular with Polish investors
Leszek Kasek
Poles remain cautious investors, with a preference for cash and bank deposits
Our recent survey paints a positive picture of Polish households’
ﬁnances. A large share have savings, many are open to investing in
capital markets for the ﬁrst time, and welcome regulatory and tax
incentives.
Against this backdrop, the new Personal Investment Account
scheme (OKI), due to come into force in 2027, could ﬁnd fertile

ground. It addresses the knowledge gap, encourages regular saving
and investing, and oﬀers tax relief. The scheme could help Poles
shift from conservative savers towards more informed investors.
An IPSOS survey commissioned by ING Bank Śląski on Poles’
attitudes towards saving and investing (a sample of 1,001
respondents), compared with selected European countries
Your privacy is important
At ING we respect your right to choose how your data is used.

You can review and change your preferences at any time in
our Cookie policy.
Essential

Targeting Cookies
Save preferences Accept all

### Page 2

(including Germany, Spain, the Netherlands and Romania), found
that Poles:
View their ﬁnancial situation more optimistically, although
they are not among the wealthiest in Europe.
Are more likely to report having savings, although these are
relatively lower than in other countries.
Prefer liquidity (cash and bank deposits) to the potential returns
oﬀered by other ﬁnancial instruments, indicating greater risk
aversion.
These conservative attitudes are borne out by ‘hard’ National

Bank of Poland data on Polish households’ ﬁnancial assets.
Are also more likely than other Europeans to describe investing
in ﬁnancial instruments as being as risky as gambling in a
casino.
Prefer to invest in their own country.
Are more likely to regard property and gold as good investment
alternatives.
Often associate saving with concerns about retirement.
Like other Europeans, they say they lack knowledge about
investing and view schemes supporting saving and investment

through government regulation and tax incentives more
favourably.
This could provide a promising environment for the Open
Investment Account scheme (OKI) in 2027. By combining tax
incentives with measures to improve ﬁnancial knowledge and
encourage regular investing, the scheme could help shift Polish
households away from a traditionally conservative approach
towards more active and informed investment.
About the survey: The survey of individuals’ saving and investment

preferences covered 11 European countries (Belgium, France, Spain,
the Netherlands, Germany, Poland, Romania, Switzerland, Türkiye,
the United Kingdom and Italy) and Australia. Samples comprised
around 1,000 people in each country and were representative in
terms of sex, age, marital status, education, labour market activity,
household size and monthly income.

### Page 3

Key ﬁndings from comparing Poland with
other European countries
In this article, we compare the survey results for Poland with the
average for European countries (Europe), as well as the results for
the Netherlands and Germany (countries wealthier than Poland),
Spain (a country similar to Poland in terms of population and GDP
per capita) and Romania (a country with lower GDP per capita than
Poland).
Poles feel better off, despite lagging
European wealth levels

75% of Polish respondents believe they are living comfortably
or doing quite well, compared with a European average of 55%
and just under 50% in Romania.
Poles assess their ﬁnancial situation optimistically, although in
2025 their stock of net ﬁnancial assets (assets minus liabilities)
relative to GDP (78% of GDP) was less than half the EU27
average (165% of GDP). Compared with 2017, net assets
increased by around 13% of GDP, but this was driven by a

decline in liabilities (deleveraging), while the stock of ﬁnancial
assets remained stable (at around 100% of GDP).
According to Eurostat data on the composition of gross
ﬁnancial assets, around half of Poles’ assets (49%, compared
with 30% in the EU27) consisted of cash and bank deposits
(current and term deposits) in 2025. The share of cash
increased markedly after the pandemic (from 9% of ﬁnancial
assets in 2017 to 11% in 2025) and was the highest among

### Page 4

the countries under review. Compared with wealthier European
countries, Poland stood out for the signiﬁcant share of loans
(5% of the total), most of which comprised retail Treasury
savings bonds. In EU statistics, these instruments are treated as
loans to the public sector rather than debt securities because of
their limited transferability (only bonds listed on the Warsaw
Stock Exchange are included in the latter category). Compared
with the EU27, Poland has a lower share of shares and other

equity in total ﬁnancial assets (19% versus 27%), as well as
investment fund shares or units (6% versus 11%), and
particularly a lower share of assets in insurance and pension
schemes (14% versus 26%).

### Page 5

Source: IPSOS survey
As many as 77% of Poles say that their household has savings,
a ﬁgure second only to the Netherlands (88%) and above the
European average (74%).
However, Poles’ savings are not substantial. 54% of
respondents report savings equivalent to no more than six
months’ net income, while wealthier Europeans have
substantially larger savings, exceeding six months’ net income.
Poles are more likely than other Europeans
to cite higher living costs as a reason for

having no savings
Source: IPSOS survey
Among respondents who report having no savings, around
60% across all countries say that they do not earn enough.
Among other factors, a relatively high proportion in Poland
(16%) cite higher living costs (compared with a European
average of 14%).

### Page 6

Compared with other countries, relatively many Poles tend to
agree that their household has too little savings to invest.
Poles favour liquidity over higher returns,
indicating greater risk aversion
Source: IPSOS survey
Around 40% of Poles say they prefer to keep their savings liquid
so that they can spend them easily if needed. This attitude
does not diﬀer substantially from the European average; the
proportions in Germany and the Netherlands are actually

signiﬁcantly higher.
Almost 70% say they do not invest in capital market
instruments such as shares, bonds, investment funds or ETFs.
This proportion is slightly higher than the European average,
but lower than in the Netherlands. Among those who said they
did not currently invest, however, Poland has the highest
proportion of respondents open to taking this step in future
(29%) (I have not invested, but I may do so one day), compared
with 24% in Europe. This indicates Poles’ openness to new

forms of investing.
Poles’ views on long-term returns reﬂect
investment caution
More than half of Poles (55%) regard gold and property as the
most proﬁtable investments, compared with a European
average of 46%. (These categories are not classiﬁed as
ﬁnancial assets in Eurostat statistics).

Source: IPSOS survey
They do not want to risk their savings or make the
achievement of speciﬁc goals dependent on investment
performance.
They believe that, for saving to be worthwhile, they should set
aside a larger share of their monthly income, at least 5% or
more. Compared with other Europeans, relatively few consider
saving just a few per cent of their monthly income (less than
5%) worthwhile.
Furthermore, Poles’ responses about their preference for a safe

investment with a small return or a riskier one with a
potentially higher return are similar to those in other countries.
However, as many as half of Poles (compared with 42% of
Europeans) see investing as akin to gambling in a casino.

Editorial 
Increasing signs of a bottom 
Strategy summary: Democrats’ thumping win in the US mid-
term elections has three key implications: i) policy gridlock is 
back in Washington, likely stalling fiscal expansion and new 
tariffs; ii) the chance of a prolonged Middle East conflict is 
curtailed, lowering oil prices and inflation expectations; and iii) 
the pace of AI data-centre buildout is at risk, curbing AI-linked 
bond supply. Higher corporate and wealth taxes are a long-term 
risk if Democrats win the 2028 presidential elections.   
For portfolios, the election reinforces our mid-cycle playbook 
already in place: remaining broadly balanced across assets, 
while looking for tactical opportunities. As the S&P500 volatility 
index (VIX) surpasses 25, we see rising signs of equity market 
bottoming. We add an opportunistic idea on the US IT sector 
(see page 4), while using elevated volatility to earn income 
through structured strategies. In bonds, we prefer the 3-7-year 
part of the Developed Market bond yield curve and BBB-to-BB-
rated corporate bonds to earn attractive income as AI supply 
eases. In FX, we position for a modestly weaker USD over 6-
12 months by allocating to ex-US assets.  
Policy implications: Oil fell 12% over the past week as 
Democrats captured the US Senate and House. The party also 
flipped governorships in five states – Georgia, Iowa, Nevada, 
Ohio and Texas – securing a majority of governorships among 
50 US states for the first time since 2010. Vice President Vance 
and Republican leaders attributed the defeat to the Iran conflict, 
making it harder for President Trump to sustain it. Democrat 
control of the defence budget from next year further constrains 
conflict escalation. Despite near-term escalation risks, we see 
the geopolitical risk premium in oil prices fading, with oil easing 
towards USD 80/bbl over 6-12 months. Oil-driven disinflation 
should allow central banks to adopt less aggressive policies. 
Adding tactical exposure to US IT sector: Semiconductors 
slumped this week amid concerns over data-centre moratoria, 
while beneficiaries of Democrat policies include defensives, 
affordability-related stocks, tariff rollback beneficiaries and the 
healthcare sector. We use the volatility to add tactical exposure 
to the US IT sector. As we enter mid-cycle dynamics, equities 
should return 8-10% annually. The 2015–2017 mid-cycle offers 
a useful comparison: with an opposition Congress, S&P 500 
returns slowed to 8.3%, but remained positive. Diversification 
across sectors and regions remains a priority amid the rotation. 
Prefer belly of the bond curve: Government bonds rallied on 
expectations of slower growth. We still expect two more Fed 
hikes by H1 2027, given sticky near-term inflation and a robust 
economy. However, cheaper oil should lower inflation towards 
the Fed’s 2% target next year, allowing rate cuts in H2 2027. 
Ultra-long yields remain elevated due to fiscal challenges. 
Bonds, particularly those from non-hyperscaler investment-
grade corporates, offer attractive income. The 3-7-year 
segment – the belly of the yield curve – offers the best balance 
of income and sensitivity to interest-rate changes. 
Credit to benefit from restrained AI supply: Since states 
control data centre regulations, Democrat wins in governorship 
races in leading data centre states, such as Texas and Georgia, 
means growth in this segment is likely to be restricted. Slower 
data-centre construction could improve credit supply-demand 
conditions due to reduced issuance by hyperscalers. However, 
some data-centre-focused deals could become less viable, 
warranting caution towards lower-quality issuers. High-yield 
credit spreads have widened, but BB- and B-rated bonds have 
held up, while CCC-rated debt has reached distressed levels. 
We prefer the BBB–BB range, spanning the boundary between 
investment-grade and high-yield debt, for income. 
A modest headwind for the USD, tailwind for gold: The 
dollar should remain range-bound near term before weakening 
modestly over 6-12 months. Democrat control of the Congress 
limits tariff escalation, fiscal expansion and rapid AI buildout, 
three pillars of US exceptionalism supporting the USD through 
2026. Disinflation should also encourage a less aggressive Fed 
stance, removing another source of USD support. Near-term 
support for the USD index (DXY) remains at 100.7. A weaker-
than-expected payrolls report tonight could accelerate the 
USD’s decline, and vice versa. A softer dollar and sustained 
central-bank buying, led by China, remain supportive of gold.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 6 November 2026

Top client questions 
  What are the equity market implications of the US mid-
term election results?   
Our view: The US mid-term election outcome adds political 
headwinds but does not alter our outlook for positive equity 
returns. Investors should maintain a Core allocation to global 
equities and continue to diversify their equity exposure across 
regions and sectors. We initiate an opportunistic idea on the US 
information technology (IT) sector, moving our opportunistic 
idea positioning from a defensive tilt to a more balanced 
positioning. Higher volatility also presents opportunities to use 
structured strategies for equity exposure.  
Rationale: The Democrats’ victory in both chambers of Congress 
has supported the outperformance of themes aligned with their 
policy priorities, with clean energy stocks, including solar 
companies, among the notable beneficiaries. Affordability-related 
themes have also benefited, including companies that could gain 
from potential tariff rollbacks. Medicaid-related stocks have also 
risen on hopes that healthcare will gain from policy changes. 
Meanwhile, semiconductor stocks remain under pressure amid 
concerns about potential moratoriums on data centres. Our AI capital 
expenditure (capex) estimates remain conservative relative to 
consensus, but with a potential reset in expectations around data 
centre spending, investors should remain selective.  
We initiate an opportunistic idea on the US IT sector after a 
significant 17% correction this quarter. Historically, this has been 
followed by strong positive returns over the next few quarters (see 
table). Semiconductors, about 40% of US IT, appears oversold after 
correcting more than 30% since we closed our opportunistic idea a 
month ago. Earnings growth for US IT continues to be solid, with 
volume driven growth in semiconductors, AI monetisation 
opportunities, ongoing cloud growth and resilient services revenue.  
As we highlighted in our November 2026 Global Market Outlook, 
the transition to mid-cycle is typically characterised by greater 
political headwinds. The mid-term election outcome reinforces this 
trend. The mid-cycle experience of 2015-17 offers a useful historical 
parallel for returns that moderate, but remain positive. Although we 
trimmed global equities to a Core allocation last week, we continue 
to expect equities to deliver mid-to-high single-digit returns per 
annum during the mid-cycle, even amid political headwinds, 
supported by steady earnings growth in both 2027 and 2028. 
Stay invested and diversify, with Overweight allocations to the US 
and AxJ, which offer the strongest earnings growth. Sector 
diversification is key as we expect market leadership to broaden. We 
are Overweight the US IT, US healthcare, US financial, US materials, 
Europe ex-UK financial and Japan financial sectors. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist

Policy uncertainty and a change in the innovation 
cycle is consistent with our view of a mid-cycle 
transition, where equity returns moderate but 
remain positive

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 6 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What are the key takeaways from Q3 2026 US earnings? 
Our view: Q3 US earnings remain robust, but more modest 
earnings beats reinforce our view that equity markets are 
entering a mid-cycle phase. We remain Overweight US equities, 
but recommend investors to maintain diversified sector 
exposure as returns moderate and market leadership broadens. 
Rationale: As of 30 October, 82% of S&P500 companies that had 
reported had beaten earnings estimates. However, aggregate 
earnings were just 4.5% above expectations, a smaller beat than in 
recent quarters. Still, Q3 US earnings growth remains robust at 
31.6% y/y, supporting expected full-year growth of 36.1% in 2026 
and 16.4% in 2027 for the S&P500. The slower pace of upgrades 
suggests the bull market is maturing as investors focus on the 
sustainability of earnings growth. This raises the hurdle for positive 
share price reactions and makes selectivity more essential. For Big 
Tech, capex commitments remain high, while free cash flow is under 
pressure and expected to remain negative. Financing risks weigh on 
sentiment, particularly for companies with weaker balance sheets.  
We remain Overweight US equities, given the strong earnings 
growth, but we expect elevated volatility and modest returns. We 
prefer quality exposure within technology while maintaining 
diversified exposure across US financials, healthcare and materials. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  What to expect from Japanese banks’ upcoming results?   
Our view: We expect Japanese banks’ earnings growth to 
continue over the medium term, supported by higher domestic 
net interest income (NII) and resilient fee income. We remain 
positive on the sector, favouring banks with strong deposit 
franchises and scope to increase shareholder returns. 
Investors will focus on whether full-year guidance upgrades 
and buybacks exceed expectations. 
Rationale: The BoJ’s September rate hike to 1.25% took effect late 
in the quarter, so its benefit should be more visible from the 
December quarter as loans reprice. We expect three further BoJ 
rate hikes by June 2027, supporting further NII expansion. We 
will also look for signs that stronger corporate investment is driving 
faster loan growth and higher financing and advisory fees.  
We prefer to place more weight on guidance upgrades driven by 
recurring lending and fee income than on those driven by gains on 
equity disposals. Continued reductions in cross-shareholdings 
could free up capital for additional buybacks, providing a further 
boost to return on equity (ROE). Key risks: faster-than-expected 
deposit rate repricing, rising credit costs and bond portfolio losses. 
—  Robin Xie, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 6 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What are the fundamental reasons behind your India 
equity upgrade? Which sectors offer favourable risk-reward? 
Our View: Indian equities offer an increasingly attractive risk-
reward profile. With India’s valuation premium to Emerging 
Markets (EMs) narrowing, foreign ownership near decade lows, 
Underweight EM positioning and a stabilising Indian rupee 
(INR), conditions are increasingly supportive of Indian equity 
outperformance vs. the broader AxJ region.  
Rationale: We have upgraded Indian equities to Overweight within 
our AxJ equities allocation. While Indian markets have witnessed 
USD 32bn of foreign investor outflows in 2026 year-to-date, strong 
domestic institutional investor inflows of USD 74bn have more 
than offset foreign selling. These outflows and the Indian equity 
market’s underperformance vs. global peers reflect broader EM 
allocation shifts towards AI, rather than weakening Indian 
fundamentals. Capital has gravitated towards markets such as the 
US, Taiwan and South Korea, which offer direct exposure to AI 
beneficiaries. However, we expect market leadership to broaden, as 
the rapid AI buildout is increasingly debt-funded, and US mid-term 
election results raise uncertainty. We prefer to diversify and we see 
investors reallocating towards structurally strong growth markets 
such as India, which offer non-AI driven growth.  
The RBI’s measures have supported INR stability, but underlying 
USD demand remains elevated. Lower oil prices would improve 
external balances, but the INR is likely to follow a path of gradual, 
controlled depreciation rather than a quick downward move. On 
valuation to its own historical average, the Nifty 50 one-year forward 
P/E is trading 18% below its long-term average while ROE at 14.7% 
is above historical averages. With India now marginally Underweight 
in large EM portfolios compared to the c.2% average Overweight 
historically, the case for a recovery in foreign flows is 
strengthening. 
India’s earnings growth remains intact. The Nifty 50 clocked Q1 
FY27 and Q2 FY27 profit-after-tax growth of c.20% y/y (ex-oil 
marketing companies). Large-caps offer greater earnings visibility 
and downside protection, while stronger growth in small- and mid-
caps warrants a selective approach given elevated valuations.  
India’s growth outlook remains supported by a strengthening 
investment cycle, manufacturing momentum, supply chain 
diversification, resilient consumption and healthy corporate and 
banking balance sheets. Importantly, earnings growth remains 
robust and broad-based across the financial, metals, telecom, 
automobiles and other domestic sectors, with Nifty 50 earnings 
growth expectations of c.14-15% over 2027 vis a vis AxJ’s 
technology driven earnings growth of 29% in 2027. 
—  Shaily Gang, Chief Investment Strategist, India

### Page 7

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 6 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Does the emerging US job market data alter your view of 
the Fed’s rate path and government bond yields? 
Our view: We do not expect the recent and incoming US job 
market data to alter our outlook unless there are significant 
deviations from the US economy’s current trajectory.  
Rationale: The US economy continues to exhibit resilience, with the 
latest Job Openings and Labour Turnover Survey data showing job 
openings surprising to the upside at 7.5mn. Initial jobless claims 
remain near three-month lows. There is little evidence of a material 
slowdown in labour demand. Inflation is expected to trend lower 
through H2 2027, but the disinflationary impulse remains a medium-
term story and is unlikely to manifest meaningfully in the near term. 
The market is now pricing in two additional 25bps hikes by H1 
2027 (down from three previously), in line with our view. These two 
rate hikes are largely priced into the US government bond yield 
curve. We estimate 2- and 10-year US government bond yields to 
range between 4.5-4.75% and 5-5.25%, respectively, over the next 
three months. Our twelve-month forecast for 2- and 10-year US 
government bond yields are 4.25-4.5% and 4.75-5%, respectively, 
The 2-year yield is already trading within our expected range and 
would only move materially higher if a significantly stronger-than-
expected payrolls print led markets to price in a third hike. With two 
hikes baked in, long-run disinflation expectations intact and an 
uncertain fiscal outlook, the longer end of the yield curve is 
expected to be less sensitive to a single payrolls release. 
—  Ray Heung, Senior Investment Strategist 
  What is your outlook for the GBP following the BoE’s rate 
hike this week? Do you see any impact on EUR/GBP? 
Our view: The BoE rate hike supports the GBP, but upside 
remains limited. We remain constructive on EUR/GBP, 
expecting a rebound towards 0.8450. 
Rationale: The BoE hiked by 25bps to 4% as expected. The GBP 
was little changed, despite hawkish inflation guidance. With markets 
pricing only around 50bps of additional BoE tightening over the next 
12 months, we see the GBP remaining vulnerable. In contrast, the 
ECB held its deposit rate at 2.50% in October but signalled readiness 
to tighten if inflation remains elevated. We expect Euro area inflation 
to rise further, keeping the ECB’s tightening bias alive. This suggests 
EUR/GBP has fallen too far relative to interest rate differentials.  
Germany’s planned increase in federal debt issuance should 
support Euro area growth, while Moody’s decision not to downgrade 
France has removed an important EUR tail risk. Meanwhile, tighter 
UK fiscal policy is likely to weigh on domestic growth and business 
investment. We expect EUR/GBP to recover towards 0.8450. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

We expect 2- and 10-year US government bond 
yields to range between 4.5-4.75% and 5-5.25%,

### Page 8

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 6 November 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Why is the AUD underperforming despite the RBA hike? 
Our view: The AUD has lagged because markets had largely 
priced in the RBA’s November rate hike to 4.85%, and the bank 
gave investors less reason to expect another. Equity weakness 
also weighed on the currency. We remain bullish on AUD/USD 
and would add on pullbacks. Australia’s interest rate advantage 
and a sustained recovery in risk sentiment should support a 
move towards 0.72 in three months and 0.74 in 12 months. 
Rationale: Ahead of the 3 November RBA rate decision, market 
pricing implied an 88% chance of a 25bps hike, leaving little scope 
for an upside surprise. What changed after the announcement 
was the pricing of the next hike. The RBA dropped its reference 
to further rate increases, and three members preferred a rate hold. 
Markets scaled back the odds of rates reaching 5.10% next year to 
around 30%, thus weighing on the AUD. 
The AUD is sensitive to swings in global risk appetite, especially 
the US tech sell-off spread to global equities, triggering margin calls 
and weighing on the currency. An equity recovery would ease that 
pressure. Even after the two 25bps Fed hikes we expect by mid-
2027, Australia would retain a 35bps rate advantage over the top 
of the Fed’s target range, supporting a gradual AUD recovery. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

We see AUD/USD upside risks on a sustained 
recovery in risk sentiment

Document 397: Top of Mind - Assessing a Less Transparent Fed

Source PDF: all report pdf/Top of Mind - Assessing a Less Transparent Fed.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs (Top of Mind)
Date: 2026-08-25
Pages: 26
Status: ok

### Page 1

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh is steering the Fed into a less transparent era. So, is that
a positive or negative development, and what could it mean for markets and the
economy? We speak with Former Fed governors Donald Kohn and Stephen Miran
as well as GS’ Jan Hatzius who agree that rigid “Odyssean” forward guidance that
commits policymakers to a pre-set path risks policy mistakes and is therefore rarely
appropriate. But they somewhat disagree about the benefits of great er transparency

around the Fed’s reaction function. Hatzius and Kohn argue that such transparency
helps markets better anticipate and price in policy changes, accelerating monetary
policy transmission. But Miran believes that the line between committing to a path
and being transparent is clearer in theory than in practice. And while GS economists
and strategists find that reduc ed transparency leads to increased market volatility,
the question is whether this just increases the market’s noise or also improves the

market’s signal. Hatzius argues largely for the former and Miran for the latter.
Jan Hatzius, Chief Economist and Head of Global Investment Research, Goldman Sachs
“Markets price what they think the Fed will do, not what they think the Fed should do. That will remain true even if the Fed
obscures its reaction function… market guesses about the Fed’s next moves will simply become worse, leading to more
volatility... and importantly, volatility that serves no constructive economic purpose.”

Donald Kohn, former Vice Chairman of the Board of Governors of the Federal Reserve System, Robert V. Roosa
Chair in International Economics and Senior Fellow, Brookings Institution
“I sympathize with Chairman Warsh to some extent. [But] the real problem with Warsh’s approach is his reluctance to articulate
how the FOMC sees the economy evolving… explaining the Fed’s thinking about the economic outlook is essential in helping
markets act as a stabilizing force and is a core component of accountability.”

ISSUE 151 | August 19, 2026 | 1:30 PM EDT
ASSESSING A LESS TRANSPARENT FED
INTERVIEWS with…
ALSO inside…
Transparency: more benefits than costs  |  Joseph Briggs and Megan Peters, GS Global Economics Research
A less transparent Fed and US rates   |  William Marshall, GS Rates Strategy Research
Fed communication and FX volatility   |  Michael Cahill and Lexi Kanter, GS FX Strategy Research
Fed: more transparent than most   |  Joseph Briggs and Megan Peters, GS Global Economics Research

Note: The following is a redacted version of the original report published August 19, 2026 [26 pgs].  All company
mentions by GS analysts are illustrative and should not be interpreted as investment recommendations.

### Page 2

El
Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research 2 
Top of Mind Issue 151

Macro news and views

US Japan 
Latest GS proprietary datapoints/major changes in views 
• No major changes in views.
Datapoints/trends we’re focused on
• US consumer spending, which we expect t o slow, with risks
skewed to the downside as the Strait of Hormuz remains
closed and further rises in gas prices would hurt consumers.
• US labor market; following weak July payroll growth,  we
estimate that the underlying pace of job growth now stands
at 5k, well below our estimate of breakeven job growth.
• US inflation, which has improved meaningfully in the past
two months, leaving us comfortable with our view that the
Fed will remain on hold through the end of the year.
Latest GS proprietary datapoints/major changes in views 
• No major changes in views.
Datapoints/trends we’re focused on
• Recent US-Japan coordinated Yen intervention, which we
think is unlikely to sustainably strengthen the Yen.
• BoJ policy; we expect the BoJ to continue hiking gradually,
with the next hike in January 2027, but the risks are skewed
toward an earlier rate hike, potentially as early as October.
• Japan consumption tax cut; while we think the tax cut will
have an only limited impact on growth, it could materially
worsen Japan’s longer-term debt outlook.
US job growth: well below breakeven 
Underlying US trend job growth, GS estimate*, thousands/month 
Japan’s debt-to-GDP: an upward sloping trajectory 
Japan public debt-to-GDP ratio, % of GDP 
*We estimate underlying trend job growth as 0.75*3-month average payroll 
growth + 0.25*9-month average household employment growth.

We provide a brief snapshot on the most important economies for the global markets

### Page 3

El
Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research 3 
Top of Mind Issue 151

New Fed Chairman Kevin Warsh is steering the Fed into a less 
transparent era characterized by shorter post-meeting 
statements, less forward guidance, and a more limited role for 
projections, marking a meaningful break from the 
transparency revolution that has defined central banking over 
the last quarter century. So, what could a less transparent Fed 
mean for markets and the economy? 
For answers, we turn to former Fed governors Donald Kohn, 
now Robert V. Roosa Chair in International Economics and 
senior fellow at the Brookings Institution, and Stephen Miran, 
now senior strategist at Hudson Bay Capital Management, as 
well as Jan Hatzius, GS’ Chief Economist and Head of Global 
Investment Research. 
We first ask whether scaling back Fed communication is a 
positive or negative development. Hatzius says it’s likely 
negative, though he draws a clear line between transparency 
and forward guidance. He argues that clarity about the Fed’s 
reaction function helps markets better anticipate policy 
changes and quickly incorporate new information into financial 
conditions, thereby accelerating monetary policy transmission 
to the economy. So, he believes that unwinding that 
transparency would be a mistake. (Note: GS Global 
Economists Joseph Briggs and Megan Peters lay out the 
evidence backing this argument on pgs. 14-15.) 
But Hatzius takes a more nuanced view of forward guidance, 
arguing that rigid “Odyssean” guidance that commits 
policymakers to a pre-set path regardless of new information 
only makes sense at the effective lower bound for the policy 
rate, while conditional “Delphic” guidance tied to the 
economic outlook is generally more defensible. Although 
markets could confuse the two, he believes that most 
sophisticated investors understand the difference and is 
therefore not particularly worried that markets have become 
overly reliant on the policy dots published in the Fed’s 
Summary of Economic Projections (SEP). Instead, he 
considers them a “useful source of additional information 
about the Fed’s reaction function” (except for the long-run 
policy dot). If anything, Hatzius would prefer the dots move 
in an even more informative direction—by publishing each 
policymaker’s combined projections for growth, inflation, 
unemployment, and the policy rate on an anonymized basis. 
Kohn agrees that clarity about the Fed’s “narrative” and 
reaction function is vital. Explaining how the Fed sees the 
economy evolving and how policy would respond, he says, is 
“essential in helping markets act as a stabilizing force and is 
a core component of accountability.” Still, he sympathizes 
somewhat with Chairman Warsh’s view that the dot plot is 
“not helpful” (see pg. 9 for Warsh’s own words on this), 
arguing that the market’s hyper-focus on the SEP’s median 
policy dot “illustrates the risk of excessive precision and 
false certainty,” as small shifts in it can trigger unwarranted 
market moves. So, he has long favored omitting the median. 
Miran, though, is more supportive of Warsh’s push for a less 
transparent Fed. Like Hatzius and Kohn, he’s concerned that 
excessive forward guidance that locks the Fed into a policy 
path raises the risk of policy errors and also argues that such 
guidance can impair market pricing of risk. But he’s less 
convinced about the benefits of increased transparency 
around the Fed’s reaction function, making the case that the 
distinction between the Fed committing to a pre-set policy 
path and being transparent about its reaction function is clear 
in theory, but less so in practice. So, he thinks “the policy dots 
absolutely have to go” but feels less strongly about omitting 
the SEP’s economic projections. 
But would less transparency allow markets to provide the Fed 
with more “direct and unfiltered” information about the 
economy, as Warsh has argued? The answer seems to boil 
down to the implications for market volatility, and whether any 
resulting volatility would simply increase the market’s noise or 
also improve its signal. 
As for the volatility implications, the evidence is fairly clear. 
Briggs and Peters find that increased central bank 
transparency has led to a reduction in market volatility over the 
last several decades. This is consistent with GS Head of US 
Rates Strategy William Marshall’s recent observation of the 
reverse: that the US rates market has already priced 
substantial uncertainty associated with a less transparent Fed, 
with the July FOMC representing the largest non-cut meeting 
day surprise in almost three decades.  
But Marshall argues that whether such meeting-day surprises 
lead to structurally higher rates volatility depends (again) on 
whether the Fed’s reaction function is well understood. If it’s 
not, he would expect higher rates volatility to persist. GS FX 
Strategists Michael Cahill and Lexi Kanter find a similar 
pattern in FX: that central bank communication shifts can 
change which events and data drive FX volatility without 
necessarily raising it, but less transparency could increase 
Dollar volatility—all else equal (though global macro 
conditions remain its biggest driver).  
But the key debate seems to be whether the increased 
volatility resulting from less Fed transparency will improve or 
worsen the market’s signal. Hatzius is clearly in the “worsen” 
camp. He argues that markets price what they think the Fed 
will do—not what it should do—and less transparency won’t 
change that; it would simply produce worse guesses. So, he 
says that while volatility in and of itself is not necessarily bad, 
volatility driven by uncertainty about the Fed’s reaction 
function would serve no constructive economic purpose. 
Kohn agrees that the lack of a clear Fed narrative could create 
damaging uncertainty. But he argues that removing overly 
specific guidance could help free markets to process new 
information more independently and isn’t too worried about 
modestly higher volatility as a result. And Miran seems most 
convinced that reducing transparency would improve the 
markets’ signal, with volatility an unavoidable but acceptable 
cost: “The first best world of signal without volatility doesn't 
exist. I'd rather have that signal, even if it's messy, than 
throw the signal out by anchoring market expectations to the 
Fed's near-term projections.” 
Allison Nathan, Editor
